# Candidate evidence ledger

Use the [scorecard](SCORECARD.md) for the cross-candidate comparison. Each entry gives the observation or construction required for full credit. The scope in each verdict applies to that candidate.

## The three OPH axioms

Full foundational contracts. A finite witness can establish a clause while common-regime completeness and refinement remain open. The PR identifiers are related ledger entries.

### A3

**Conditional maximum randomness.** From the repair and record grammar supply the feasible family, faithful reference and positive weights. Realize its unique weighted relative-entropy minimizer on the jointly separating accessible cover with exact constraint satisfaction and compatibility under overlap and refinement.

Criterion · [axiom_registry.yaml](../../claims/axiom_registry.yaml) · [AXIOM_REFERENCE.md](../../docs/AXIOM_REFERENCE.md)

- **Interval Echo · Partial.** Two overlapping two-cell observers realize the unique accessible minimizer in both orders for a frozen faithful reference, weights and constraints. Selection and refinement of general source-derived covers and constraints remain open. A separately specified finite comparison service generates stationary probabilities for five local record modes. Its signed comparator, standard cell and deployment rule are supplied. The existing cover and refinement obligations still apply. The marked-face address experiment realizes a faithful unique entropy optimizer whose four-child projection is exactly the preceding optimizer at every finite depth of its specified construction. Native records cover the 80- and 320-cell boards. A changed controller preserves every agreement constraint and increases the depth-two objective by 0.117282 nats. At the same four-child refinement, four native phase words prepare the unique fine relative-entropy minimizer I₄/4 ⊗ ρ at any fixed complete signed coarse marginal ρ and matched product reference. The coherent lift has identical coarse histories and log(4) fewer nats. At child coherence 1/2 the entropy derivative is −3 log(5)/4 with that coarse state fixed. On an eight-port inter-owner collar a fixed-duration native shuffle realizes the relative-entropy projection at every parity moment as well as full-fibre conditional resampling using the same faithful reference. All 70 sharp starts are covered. Seven selector exposures and two starting textures on each of three boards check the visible probabilities. The finite collar specifies its controllers, observable menu and reference preparation. Padded fair-bit histories give a faithful multiplicity-weighted reference and compatible refinements. At depth three the public position path distinguishes 21 histories while the complete source transcript distinguishes 64. A separate native mean-one constraint has an irrational optimizer. No bounded fair sampler attains it exactly. An extensible comparison controller attains it almost surely in three expected reads; a 12-read replay retains both unresolved prefixes. ## Finite theorem premises and executable witnesses

Each bounded row names its source, menu and sector. The confluence, public probability and global gauge rows make explicit the additional premises needed to transfer those witnesses.

### FT-SEAM

**Exact directed seam write.** On the blank seam register preserve total integer load, minimize the two squared endpoint loads, use a load-independent fair tie bit, and respect endpoint exchange. Verify the exact decrease for strict classical writes.

- **Contrast memory · Demonstrated.** The directed write puts the endpoints at floor/ceiling of half the total and stores contrast and tie. The bounded-load polynomial settling estimate covers strict classical moves. ### FT-RECORD

**Orthogonal local pointer records.** For a declared finite reader, implement distinguishable orthogonal outcome labels retained in physical registers with a certain immediate reread on the same record.

Register crosswalk · PR-02

Criterion · [executable_observer_construction.tex](../../paper/executable_observer_construction.tex) · [NoBroadcastingAdapter.lean](../../Lean/EventAlgebra/NoBroadcastingAdapter.lean)

- **Interval Echo · Demonstrated.** The cell-patch clock reads a sharp material rail; repeated protected readings of the reported states agree. Both owners reread the same physical digit in the projection test. For one fresh write in the 78-word total-two sector, the native reduced Interval Echo channel equals the historical E record law on all 6,084 matrix units. A total-sensitive control equals T. All sharp words agree and 110 matrix units distinguish the laws. A four-port empty/full preparation gives 786,711 returns in 1,048,576 simulated starts at the held phase π/3, versus 524,560 with the control. Routing the spectators to the original historical ports (2,3) gives a held bright fraction of 0.748930 against 0.750314 predicted from the other ports. This settles the model-side empty/full multiplier; physical E/T selection requires independent laboratory records. On the historical empty/single preparation the native contrast record gives a bright probability of 3/8 at every phase. The coin-and-current control gives 5/8 and 1/8 at opposite phases. All 78 terminal words and nine held forecasts were checked across 5,505,024 launches. ### FT-CONFLUENCE

**Consensus on the declared observation quotient.** Declare the repair relation and protected record equivalence. Prove completeness of normal forms for consistency, joinability of every overlap fork on that quotient, and strictly well-founded descent for accepted nontrivial transactions.

Register crosswalk · PR-01

Criterion · [reality_as_consensus_protocol.tex](../../paper/reality_as_consensus_protocol.tex) · [ObserverConfluence.lean](../../Lean/ObservableNormalForms/ObservableNormalForms/ObserverConfluence.lean) · [OBSERVABLES.json](../../docs/registers/OBSERVABLES.json)

- **Interval Echo** · **Demonstrated** · [Unique minimum-load census after routed repair on the federation](../experiments/interval_echo_consensus/routed_counts/README.md). Occupied and ready flags determine the consistent asynchronous sharp endpoint. All 284 one-step forks on 19,683 path states join. For one protected coherent sweep, stored local priorities order conflicting visits. Disjoint visits commute, giving exactly the same full addressed instrument for every legal schedule, including arbitrary untouched references. Five legal schedules agree on 3,888 path inputs. Removing precedence changes 192 endpoints. With free flight and one sharp double, even a fixed priority sweep gives different permanent addresses when the same flight and sweep are interchanged. At a quarter exchange time the probability of a different address is at least 529/16384 throughout the degree-five/six federation family. Uniform random seam attempts terminate almost surely on this sector. Protected closed vacancy walks generate all excess-position permutations. The largest linear observation algebra forgetting every closed transport is the span of the uniform-position projector and its complement. Counting a distinguishable reversible loop and its inverse as strict repairs makes a two-step descent cycle. Every weakly fair guarded request schedule gives the same full terminal isometry on a fixed initial joint public flag eigenspace, including coherent inputs entangled with a spectator. On arbitrary coherent inputs the occupied-address and untouched-spectator instrument is schedule-independent. Across different initial flags a premature probe can erase a relative phase. Free flight still changes some protected endpoints. At total load two and blank collars, a full priority sweep writes one mark for a double and none for two singles, in either order with all-open flight. The three-state quotient has completeness, strict descent and a unique consistent endpoint for each of its two protected values. On the doubled sector of the 20-, 80- and 320-cell screens, scalar effects are the only fixed terminal effects giving the same input probabilities for both orders at every sufficiently small exposure. The address and coherent sign record can distinguish the schedules. The routed strict-write service discharges all normal-form premises on the public vacancy, double and occupied-seam census. It admits arbitrary ternary loads, protected initialized routing capacity, and arbitrary native flight between transactions. Each accepted write consumes one vacancy and one double, adds one mark and lowers excess quadratic load by two. Every competing successful request has the same census successor. Normality is equivalent to attaining the global quadratic-load minimum at fixed load. The two invariants, vacancies plus marks and doubles plus marks, identify every normal endpoint on this quotient. An uninterrupted scan of all 28,680 pairs of 240 ports, starting with eleven occupied collars, made 74 further writes in either order and attained the minimum 224. Positions, signs, arbitrary dirty routing trees and the joint clock history lie outside this conclusion. If the pair is freshly uniform after each preceding flight, the exact write probability at fixed census is vd/C, where C counts unordered port pairs. The time to completion is a sum of geometric waits with mean strictly below N(N−1) requests on N ports. Every refused request counts. All 16 four-port count banks on sharp or coherent starts agree with the independent forecast. A finite deadline has an explicit risk bound. A protected deterministic scan permits at most C requests and parks free flight during that scan. These are bounds for this apparatus and observable. ### FT-STRICT-WEDGE

**Strict repair with an order-independent endpoint and a retained phase on an intercell wedge.** Realize strictly descending accepted writes and a common endpoint channel for all blank-collar two-unit inputs on two competing native seams, including coherences. At a fixed winning address perform a local phase read after the write.

Register crosswalk · PR-01

Criterion · [PROTOCOL.md](../experiments/interval_echo_consensus/PROTOCOL.md) · [FOLLOWUP.md](../experiments/interval_echo_consensus/FOLLOWUP.md)

- **Interval Echo · Demonstrated.** A buffered two-request priority service on two cells tests absolute written contrast. It excludes balanced inputs, accepts only energy-lowering writes, and gives identical channels under reversed arrivals. Relative-phase preparations give rail probabilities 0 and 1; the control which interrogates both seams gives 1/2 for both preparations. A weak doublet, a Standard Model loop/flux action, and a common faithful representation with the Standard Model global quotient remain to be realized. In the registered one-load colour representation the leading arrival from one cell has one-quarter triplet weight. A reference-tagged orbital remains coherent after singlet projections at both endpoints for both material signs under the specified extension of this meter. Applying identical pulse strengths to the one-load and two-load bound-pair triplets gives a 23-dimensional joint connected current image. The full central turn that is identity on the pair moves the one-load launch off both input ports. These two registered carriers do not realize a common twelve-dimensional action at those strengths. On a unit-filled material carrying a vacancy and one excess, the protected π-pulse loop image is exactly S_m for m=11,239,959,3839. The mark representation is faithful and only identity acts as a scalar; the free leading crossing words have scalar kernel {I,-I}. This source-specific loop action has trivial permutation center and does not realize the required Z6 gluing-to-kernel identity. In the joint address action, a pair-source line defines an orthogonal plane supporting a controllable two-state rotation. With the rotation triplets held fixed, the native uniform circle and the independent colour and plane controls have faithful image SU(3) × SU(2) × U(1). Imposing equality of the bright-line phase and the colour determinant selects a different subgroup with diagonal order-six covering kernel. An allowed antipodal pulse has exact colour determinant i and a measured pair bright cosine between −0.656 and −0.622. This excludes every continuous single-valued colour character as the phase rule for all line-preserving native controls. The selection of a physical loop and global form is unresolved. Both modes of the registered pair plane transmit into the adjacent plane on an all-open 20-cell screen except at isolated exposures. A launch phase changes 29 of 30 receiving seam frequencies at exposure 32. An exact calculation proves unequal mode efficiencies outside a discrete set. A single bridge between two isolated cells blocks both modes by fivefold symmetry. The global gauge identification requires compatible transports and a common action on matter. Two coherent remote apertures with a signed turnaround give the same successful rotation for every input in the registered pair plane. The home observer measured a relative path phase of 0.458 radians (joint interval 0.360–0.563). For any coherent aperture and any antisymmetric turnaround K, reciprocity makes the two-dimensional return matrix TᵀKT a scalar times the same antisymmetric matrix. This controlled family has commuting projective image and cannot select the order-six global form. On connected protected blank ports native contacts give independent special-unitary blocks and a two-parameter determinant torus; a four-port two-load meter has a cyclic ambiguity of order 59 resolved by a single-load read. On the bound trimer four held three-label tables match an independent inverse-word calibration. The phases on one arm and the complementary pair are conjugate and cancel across the three arms. The physical six-axis gluing and full electroweak kernel require a separate attachment. ## Einstein realization on one physical source

G1–G6 are the six hypotheses for a common source, state and refinement tower. GR-NULL records the null-direction route. The paper also gives a quantitative route using ten timelike observers with matched bulk, area and stationarity errors o(ℓ⁴); it requires no separately assumed null-balance equation.

**Lorentzian event and geometry domain.** On one candidate-generated refinement tower supply an authenticated causal order, event placement and count/volume limits that agree on a local differentiable, stably causal Lorentzian domain.

- **Interval Echo** · **Demonstrated** · [Regional modular energy predicted from repair across the boundary](../experiments/interval_echo_einstein_station/PAIR.md). Full criterion · Partial. Six charge variations on a prepared 79-state cap agreed with an independent weighted seam-energy meter to 0.00000951 exchange units. With fresh simulated counts the held port-9 difference was +0.01377 ± 0.02042 units. The cap weights, coherent filters and reference were supplied. The isolated seam standard also fails the three-port Gibbs overlap test. Geometric normalization and one directional matter stress across caps remain open. Restrictions of one prepared state agree on a four-port overlap. On a held six-port charge, tracing the independently specified parent Gibbs operator gives a discrepancy of +0.00063 ± 0.02196 native units. Keeping only the weighted interior seams gives +0.56132 ± 0.02010. The induced regional generator contains a coupling between nonadjacent ports. With one rotation-averaged native reference the twelve overlapping star logarithms restrict to angular quadratics on the constant mode and slow triplet. Nine readings predict three independent held readings at two exposures on two preparations. Unrestricted one-load preparations can violate this closure. On the four-mode sector, real modular readings at all isolated exposures identify twelve of the fifteen normalized state variations. Three independent oriented seam readings recover the remaining information and predict energy transport to neighbouring cells. Separate orbital-exchange and inverse-echo seam meters identify a one-unit source energy increment. Moving that increment from the orbital to the reversal parity of the marks changes the probe channel at equal measured regional energy and zero mean current. A common tensor-stress reading needs independent strain and flux calibration. For two formed seam heads with fixed marginals, the independently specified regional observation of a pairing flag determines the signed modular variation. Including the flag gives a linear response in its admission probability, and excluding it gives a quadratic response with a finite coefficient. The physical cap boost and ordinary-stress calibration are separate inputs. Two thermal cell transfers with the same mean energy change on every seam have eight-port modular responses differing by 0.149669 nats per unit transfer. The smooth-source moment bound requires any missed response to vanish faster than the fourth power of proper radius. On the connected binary Gibbs branch, exact thermal screening through any finite buffer would forbid the observed one-load propagation. Replacing the core with an independent copy of its marginal gives signed regional-minus-global error at most minus the reference conditional mutual information. Across a full neighbor shell on one cell the error is −0.000147181 nats, with conditional information 0.0000456504. Bounds on both signs and on the finite-graph boundary series do not imply the requisite uniform small-ball rate. A common homogeneous modular gain at two proper radii incurs at least one third relative error against the diamond scaling. Opposite proper exit times recover the diamond weight with integrated error O(Cℓ⁶+hℓ³) on an independently supplied chart. A calibrated three-outcome return reads weighted energy on a finite hopping guide, with a separate sharp-position or hopping measurement. Changing the terminal register can change its modular generator. On the finite scalar comparison the native one-particle program and the supplied Gaussian vacuum have distinct regional algebras. During free flight an energy read of the actual receiving seam detects the collar. Sorting many copies with supplied cyclic control gives a thermodynamic energy derivative exactly proportional to full entropy at the reference. A physical regional stress requires distinct local and directional identification. On a separately prepared rotation-averaged cell, spoke and rim energies represent every nonconstant angular component of the twelve regional modular variations on the constant-plus-triplet one-load sector. The rim has the larger weight. Integrating out the stored heads gives a coherent self-energy supported at the four marked ports. Both material charges give a connected delayed hopping modulus with pairing-to-ordinary response ratio 0.29682–0.29744, distinct from the dark-port variance ratio 0.53140–0.53583 and head entropy ratio 0.56432–0.56454. A held all-twelve-range-invisible correlation transfer changes the independently read four-tick full material force for both charges. Its certified slopes are (1.25430–1.25464)×10⁻⁸ for vacancy and (4.89110–4.89128)×10⁻⁷ for excess in native units per transfer. On the complete normalized two-parity head algebra, 31 charge-hidden return-time probabilities of the existing radar identify all 31 state coordinates, including parity and both marginals. Independently calibrated linear scores predict the two absolute marked material forces from those timestamps for every state in this algebra. The positive regional state on the central bridge gives the exact signed coefficient of strain work in its modular first law. Holding the other three regional controls fixed gives 0.67421–0.67593 nats per work unit for vacancy and 0.98443–0.98601 for excess. The untagged four-terminal algebra includes a common outside mode and separate occupied vacancy and excess blocks. Its conditional modes carry an entropy response even at fixed sector weights and fixed bridge energy currents. - **Interval Echo** · **Demonstrated** · [Patch energy continuity and the change in a seam current](../experiments/interval_echo_null_balance/README.md). Full criterion · Partial. The freely evolving sphere obeys exact additive patch energy continuity. Twelve scalar tests agree to 2.7×10⁻⁸ native units. Independently sampled one-load circulation changes at the measured rate, with a nonzero residual between the owners of a shared gateway. No nonzero time-reversal-odd occupation current supported on single native seams commutes with the full one-load generator in the certified family. A low-energy covariant stress and its connection remain open. Two conjugate triplet preparations have identical isolated real cap records and opposite repair currents. Their released neighbouring patch energies differ by 8.3 combined standard errors. Adding three oriented seam readings to the regional readout predicts the held difference on this sector. With independent preparation tags on each of 20 ancestors, a native connected modular charge divided by the fourth power of routed radius gives 0.001713 and 0.001761 nats in home-range units on the two finest boards, and 0.001765 on doubling the finest radius. Far disjoint annuli have zero mixed charge. Near disjoint annuli have a mixed contrast of (0.9703 ± 0.0883) × 10⁻⁹ nats. Ordered pair admissions account for the scaling. The routed lengths bypass the sources and the tag and modular reference are supplied. On the programmed resonant guide, arbitrary complex wave packets have exact local number and symmetrized energy continuity. The stationary profile at the scattering pole has a normalized inverse-square core on a fixed periodic volume. The source rest energy is declared. Physical momentum, pressure and a covariant Ward identity on observer spacetime remain open. On a translated twelve-cell region containing both heads, energy and directed shift charge have separate exact local rate equations. The latter has independently measured boundary flux and marked-contact force. Two-cell shift coherences are orthogonal to every linear combination of one-step bridge energy currents. These identities do not yield a symmetric physical stress in a proper frame. For the measured force F and native H, Tr(H⁷F)=1/8 in each charge and parity sector. No additive change of the momentum observable can absorb this force while preserving the same boundary flux. On orbitals uniform inside each cell, 119 unmarked bridges have energy current exactly 40 times load current. All 79 relative weak-phase directions give matching instantaneous translation and routed flow responses. At fixed phase their collar derivatives vanish and the subspace leaks under free flight. The shift distinction and the nonzero force moment survive restriction to the exact 2,912-dimensional transmitting sector in each charge and parity block. The full material body force on the registered translation chart is separately recoverable from eight delayed local interference measurements. At the earlier appointment two preparations with matching measured stress and optical derivatives give different forces. The terminal material currents and their commutators with the native Hamiltonian reconstruct the sharp optical image and its first two time derivatives on this source. At the same source appointment the signed sharp-screen expansion, energy current and focusing of the vacancy, excess and actual fair-pooled images have a nonzero determinant. Every common clock and common scalar flux conversion leaves an odd focusing residual above 0.1229 in native units. With arbitrary constant baselines, the response equations also fail under affine forward stress pooling. A one-image test excludes clocks built from the two running bridge occupations at fixed flux conversion. ### G4

**Stationary generalized entropy at fixed proper volume.** For the same region and state establish the edge-center entropy split and physical area normalization, modular first law, and vanishing first variation of generalized entropy at fixed proper volume.

Register crosswalk · PR-26, PR-27, PR-28

Criterion · [recovering_observer_spacetime_and_einstein_dynamics_from_overlap_consistency.tex](../../paper/recovering_observer_spacetime_and_einstein_dynamics_from_overlap_consistency.tex) · [OBSERVABLES.json](../../docs/registers/OBSERVABLES.json)

- **Interval Echo** · **Demonstrated** · [Unique global entropy-plus-area maximum for one or arbitrarily many four-station surveys](../../code/matched_einstein_limit/source_implication/full_algebra/PROOF.md). Full criterion · Partial. The regional first law and number-sector split have finite measurements. For an interior swap mixed into the same prepared cap as G2, the entropy slope is +0.0000344891 nats per unit mixture. The complete instantaneous gateway state and every total-load probability stay fixed. The commuting control has zero slope. A physical area that responds, a measured fixed proper volume and vacuum stationarity on shrinking regions remain open. Two stationary interior modes have identical full exterior one-load histories. Against the prepared cap their first-law slopes differ by 0.4410 nats, with a sampled difference of 0.4352 ± 0.0089. A second load entering the region distinguishes them. On a 20-cell sphere a local hand times first-hit out-and-back flights among six sites. All fifteen held-pair tests fail the Euclidean anchor-consistency domain. The entire enrolled radar law is identical across oriented ternary source histories with a nonzero interior register-entropy tangent. These readings cannot identify a fixed proper volume or an area response. Six stations on the two-write preparation also exclude treating their conditional hand ranges as minimizing distances in a convex weakly curved three-dimensional region. All 24 five-station checks require a fourth tangent direction over the enrolled curvature interval, including the source-free control. Independent native repair coins select a joint entropy maximum on every feasible tangent of the three-cell record algebra. Correlated coins preserve the owner tables and instantaneous cut and give an entropy slope of −ln 5 per triple, including 93 independent triples in a connected 279-cell region. Free equal-energy orbital mixtures have identical passive boundary histories and entropy slope −ln 3 at a three-to-one mixture. On another source a mixture preserving first-return tetrahedral volume changes the volume inferred by the free-pulse ruler. Both rulers miss a withheld path by over 50%. On nine monitored two-load regions an exterior observer reads the conditional boundary capacity. Its entropy gradient cancels the measured interior gradient within 0.02583 nats on 54 held transfers. Nine jointly energy- and cut-load-preserving variations cancel within 0.00373. A coherent probe across two gateways exposes a stationary orbital phase in joint sharp occupations at 37.6 to 38.8 standard errors. Reciprocal launch volume and aperture give relative cubed-area to squared-volume ratios of 1, 0.5 and 0.25. At equal launch volume, changing the shape on the same release or varying matter at fixed shape defeats the calibrated aperture as a compensating surface term. Across 24 shape and matter comparisons an independently read exterior-bit coefficient leaves at most 0.00604 nats. The physical area coefficient, common proper volume and autonomous vacuum selection require independent evidence. In equal-launch-volume shape probes on the 327,680-cell host, an independently read available transmission aperture recovered 1.374 and 1.377 nats per additional contact at half filling. The coefficient varies with filling. A matched five-class variation changes entropy at fixed mean native energy and complete two-tag flags. Material-hand route balls have doubling dimension 1.999 at the finest scale. Freely stationary equal-energy preparations differ in interior entropy at fixed exterior state and independently surveyed mean hull volume and area. None of the enrolled rulers supplies a three-dimensional proper ball on the physical source. Fitting a curved homogeneous three-space separately to four native route-volume ratios requires at least 6.42% to 13.70% changes in held distances. The curvature fixed on 1,280 cells misses the two finer volume ratios by 66.55% and 91.76%. These blank-host records exclude the proposed identification of this routed volume and distance with a common three-dimensional constant-curvature ruler. A mobile vacancy or excess creates a faithful two-seam sharp reference at exposure 4. Under continued native coupling the instantaneous joint entropy slopes are −4.14007 and +4.29523 nats per exposure on 20 cells, and +1.13942 and −1.09493 on 80 cells. Both marginal slopes also resolve at two symmetric exposure offsets. On removing the source the restored uniform material gives a scalar two-head Hamiltonian that freezes every generated state. This tests time stationarity of that preparation. Fixed-proper-volume variation of generalized entropy, including its area term, still requires an independent geometric instrument. Transfers among nine ordered source words preserve both reconstructed tetrahedral volumes and either chosen area. Their entropy slopes are +0.1769704 and +0.1463704 nats on 20 cells. At the uniform word reference entropy is stationary in all directions and both candidate areas have a nonzero gradient at jointly fixed volume. The finite completion-count identity states precisely which area calibration would imply generalized stationarity. On a two-dimensional surface of fixed volumes and fixed word marginals, a positive charge slope of 0.161753 nats increases both inferred tetrahedral areas. No nondecreasing differentiable combination of these areas can cancel the charge. Another transfer gives opposite area signs for the two rulers. These are calibrated population responses. Even at 2⁶⁰ simulated releases per edge the direct timing area contrasts are unresolved. A protected annular route instead gives zero geometric contrast with a resolved signed charge. A four-port transfer keeps the complete six-port shell and every three-port marginal equal between the arms while changing the eight-port modular charge. The fixed-volume calibration determinant tests other area instruments on the same transfers. In the whole-cell Gibbs source, conditional mutual information provides a signed leakage certificate with independently evaluated perturbation cost. Every two-port state and the full exterior can be fixed while the eight-port regional entropy changes. The gravity test requires a proper-area response to the same variations. At the gain measured from a separate ordinary preparation (23.1042362), a native interval certificate establishes a realizable strict entropy-plus-area maximum at fixed survey volume on the entire eight-dimensional head support. An ordinary energy change and a fixed-energy, fixed-marginal correlation change raise entropy by 0.409453 and 0.231106 and reduce matched-volume area by 0.0177220 and 0.0100028, respectively, per unit mixing probability. The freely formed and selected heads give distinct independent right-hand rail readings (0.020107 and 0.507974). A smaller preparation family can appear stationary but fail a range-invisible native transfer. A different timeout on the full maximum gives residual 0.7264. A programmed flat ball at changing energy has constant area. The state-selection law, proper geometry and uniform macroscopic residual remain open. A finite sum over matched proper-volume shells with separately bounded clock and entropy errors proves a contraction criterion. At the single equilibrium native volume varies under rotations preserving the full entropy, marginals and energy. For the two-word routed collar at every resolved scale, normalized stationarity along tag correlations holds exactly in the limit of the independent-tag reference. This removes the normalized linear collar signal. At a fixed nonzero correlation change the nonlinear relative-entropy cost is of the same fourth order in radius as the retained linear signal. For the 80-cell four-station survey, the prepared equilibrium uniquely maximizes entropy plus ordinary-calibrated area on the entire measured volume level, with a gap of at least 0.14 times squared trace-norm distance. A different faithful native preparation at the same volume gives a fixed-volume derivative between 7.47 and 7.49 nats per unit odd fraction. A contracting tracial ensemble procedure selects its native preparation probabilities. The global objective theorem extends to every number of disjoint copies under pooled ranges or separately summed survey areas and volumes, including correlated and entangled input states. On a connected 80-cell board, uniformly supplied short flights converge in each parity sector to the tracial coin state. At the unique parity matching the survey volume its fixed-marginal, fixed-energy correlation transfer has generalized-entropy slope between −0.004816 and −0.004815. Also every one of the 24 actual capped radar settings decreases left-coin X at the variational optimum if the heads are reused. Repeated equal use of the 24 radar settings converges at fixed parity. At its independently matched volume, two native changes of correlations preserve ordinary energy and both head marginals, but require disjoint entropy/area gains. An exact local path keeps area and volume fixed while changing entropy. At the long-run state there is a reachable entropy-increasing transfer leaving all twelve directed capped mean ranges, ordinary energy, parity and both one-head marginals exactly fixed. A joint Y⊗Y reading distinguishes the states. With the parity mass and both one-head marginals in each parity sector fixed, six additional native joint Pauli means and the twelve mean ranges identify the full eighteen-coordinate correlation state and its entropy. Six extra scalar linear readings are minimal on this slice. On the complete normalized two-parity head algebra, 31 charge-hidden return-time probabilities of the existing radar identify all 31 state coordinates, including parity and both marginals. Independently calibrated linear scores predict the two absolute marked material forces from those timestamps for every state in this algebra. The twelve pooled range means and both independently calibrated marked forces together have rank fourteen on the eighteen-dimensional correlation slice. A native change leaves all fourteen exactly fixed while increasing entropy at the volume-matched radar equilibrium. The central bridge has a literal five-mode matter region after the probe charge is discarded. Its modular entropy derivative is read from local work, occupancy, imbalance and current. One allowed change holds both complete marked forces and all twelve pooled ranges fixed, raises both bridge work means equally, and increases this untagged regional entropy by 4.93893 to 4.94977 × 10⁻⁷ nats at displacement 0.01. Its surveyed tetrahedral area and volume are constant. On the volume-matched 80-cell source an allowed change holds the four-station radar geometry, both bridge energy currents, the two-port state and all three four-port number-sector probabilities exactly fixed. The untagged four-register entropy derivative still exceeds 3.2465 × 10⁻⁷ nats per unit source. Thirteen independent simulated tomography settings resolve the finite change. This excludes stationarity for this regional assignment at every area coefficient. On the same 26-fixed number-sector control the four loads with both head memories have an entropy derivative greater than 6.99 × 10⁻⁶ nats per unit, and the full closed flight including all load positions and both heads has derivative greater than 1.41 × 10⁻⁵. The heads’ conditional entropy reinforces the four-port discrepancy. On the native 80-cell two-head source, independently attracting preparations obtained by changing the route probabilities agree to the certified precision of all six tetrahedron lengths under one fixed survey. The full-flight entropy rises by about 5.95 × 10⁻⁶ nats and the signed bridge energy current changes by about −0.000533. An independent parked-head detector distinguishes the endpoints. Putting both sources on the same route schedule separates their volumes after one cycle. A central-current gain calibrated on another pair overpredicts their second volume difference by a factor of 24. This four-station geometry and the common parked energy leave the source unspecified across the supplied baths. Fixed-bath stationarity on proper shrinking regions and a common null stress relation remain open. A four-station survey commissioned from 62 head standards predicts 720 directed ranges and 60 independent volume readings on twelve held preparations through eight common cycles. Two further preparations agree within the simultaneous sensitivity of all twelve present ranges. After four cycles six ranges distinguish them and their independent volume intervals are disjoint. The parked head records carry predictive information beyond the initial ranges. Each setting uses 2^44 simulated launches. - **Interval Echo** · **Demonstrated** · [Nine directions predict regional charge on the constant mode and slow triplet](../experiments/interval_echo_angular/README.md). Full criterion · Partial. A finite patch recovered its rank-three angular frame in all 32 high-budget count tables. Under a common orthonormal-frame null lift, 160 of 220 nine-port subsets span the required nine contractions. Every eleven-port subset does. Averaging opposite directions retains six. Directly assigning pair gauge currents to stress still fails the tensor test. Physical null directions, regional stress and shrinking-ball remainders remain open. Nested regions of 6, 11 and 12 ports now share a compatible state derived from a common cell. Their chord radii have a supplied scale. Proper shrinking radii and a controlled physical curvature remainder are open. For the two represented finite cell references, an operator bound transfers to every subset of the twelve ports, at most 0.001443 native energy units for a normalized-state contrast. This bounds service approximation on one cell only. For a rotation-averaged reference, normalized state variations on the constant mode and slow triplet give all nine angular responses. Nine prespecified directions predict the other three within 2.60 propagated standard errors over twelve independent held settings. The same reconstruction misses a freely released sharp-port control by 5.99 nats at exposure 0.75. The physical stress and small-ball identifications require distinct measurements. A finite nonzero collar stress on a shrinking proper-ball family requires a leading signed collar energy proportional to the fourth power of the measured radius. Only the residual after subtracting that coefficient may be discarded in the Einstein balance. The two-write apparatus provides no independently measured proper radius for this limit. After grouping sixteen freely evolving cells per region, a nine-direction forecast misses three held modular directions by 75.2% of the angular variation. The optimal fit to all twelve has a 14.2% relative residual in this supplied frame. On 1,280 and 5,120 cells the held forecast errors are 42.5% and 73.3% of the angular variation. Absolute errors fall, while relative errors turn upward. Fitting a curved homogeneous three-space separately to four native route-volume ratios requires at least 6.42% to 13.70% changes in held distances. The curvature fixed on 1,280 cells misses the two finer volume ratios by 66.55% and 91.76%. These blank-host records exclude the proposed identification of this routed volume and distance with a common three-dimensional constant-curvature ruler. On the finest clocked-ball census the four formed collar sources give unflagged modular doubling exponents 3.977–3.996, compared with 1.9994 when the observer keeps the admission record. The finite resolvent bound holds for every pairing probability at fixed source. Physical proper radius and a common refinement of source and instrument are unverified. First-exit product errors in supplied smooth convex rest balls cost O(Cℓ⁶+hℓ³) at bounded energy density. At radius 1/16 three transverse directions in a tilted frame of the curved product chart recover 1.000405 versus area contraction 1.000132. Extending this to native clocks in a common dynamical metric and all required timelike frames remains open. The earliest five-by-five screen image has one correlation-dependent transverse component. At four exchange times both angular components are resolved on the same source. The screen has no independent null affine calibration. Equal-weight star counts on a native twelve-port cell have rank one on even angular responses. Double pole weight restores rank six exactly. In static geometry a proper directional shell array and independent rim clock rates then determine every even null Ricci value with area error o(r⁴) and rate error o(r²). The native angular experiment distinguishes two stationary orbitals. Its sector area and clocks have no common-source proper calibration. On the 80-cell survey board two post-flight phase controls give identical registered local stress and instantaneous optical screen derivatives through order eight, yet different native marked forces. A later interference measurement distinguishes their coherence. Eight delayed local measurements on four receiver ports reconstruct the full material marked force at population precision for both changes and both charges. Full criterion · Open. A free load is exactly insensitive to the energy of an antisymmetric memory coin. A symmetric occupied contact scatters a second load. A local 7–8 hand resolves a signed change at fixed ordinary occupancy of +3.835 millionths of a hand unit (simultaneous 99% interval 1.705–5.964), against +3.744 on three screen sizes at matched microscopic addresses. Two terminal probabilities exclude a scalar multiple of the ordinary-load intervention. A single written word hides the between-history fluctuation from tag-independent flights. Two distinct writes under a shared tag change a tag-independent arrival probability by (1.034 ± 0.214) × 10⁻⁹. The sign of their connected modular-energy contrast depends on the physical reference. The initial projected relative acceleration shifts by +9.68963 on adding ordinary occupancy and exactly zero on changing the between-history variance, by the native double commutator. In the six-station study, the mean ranges among returns fail three-dimensional weak-curvature comparison even in the vacant arm. A common physical reference, matched stress normalization, independently responsive geometry and shared positive Newton constant are open. With one open gateway, all home launches have identical normalized exterior intensity patterns at each fixed frequency. Transporting the sharp tag with five native swaps opens a second gateway. Held launches then reach the two receivers in distinct proportions. One normalized frequency response changes by −0.00651 under ordinary occupancy, while the history contrast is at the numerical comparison scale. The two co-located hands agree in vacuum. Following a release across one boundary the occupied meter lags the empty one by about 0.00028 radians on the 20-, 80- and 320-cell screens. The lag follows from double occupation of the meter contact. This readout distinguishes a probe-dependent lag from a common gravitational lapse. Unit and double material tags have exactly matched free flights on three guarded federations. A 32-unit seam source reduces unit-tag entry by 22.44–27.52 percentage points at two ticks, with exact sham motion for the double tag. A different supplied internal matching increases exterior probability for both tags by about five points. Free native evolution conserves every blank/written seam flag. On the native four-tracer instrument the shared and fresh collar words have equal exact initial focusing. Their measured contrast is −0.00395 ± 0.06467 at simultaneous 99% coverage conditional on the clock, compared with an ordinary-load focusing contrast of −27.65519 in release-volume units.   - **Homogeneous Bianchi I Einstein–scalar source** · One calibration of the scalar mass squared, matter coupling and vacuum term predicts six held diagonal Einstein–scalar measurements. The largest Einstein-component error is 0.000279 in source units. Suppressing matter feedback raises the error to 0.5884. This comparison supplies the action, homogeneous geometry, scalar meter and proper clock. [Comparison study](../../code/matched_einstein_limit/README.md)
  - **Homogeneous semiclassical Einstein geometry coupled to six quantized real scalar oscillators** · After one calibration, quantum pressure and radar give held diagonal Einstein components within 0.00000559 at the finest appointment spacing. Independent source quadratures predict later radar records within 0.00000999 normalized error. The comparison supplies the metric dynamics, quantum matter Hamiltonian and clock. [Comparison study](../../code/matched_einstein_limit/quantum_scalar_radar/README.md)
  - **Homogeneous semiclassical Einstein geometry coupled to six quantized real scalar oscillators** · The independently measured scalar energy and pressures predict the volume-derived Ricci component within 0.000004993 at finest radar spacing. Even contracting flat rays have raw volume acceleration near −14.20, which the measured expansion correction removes. [Comparison study](../../code/matched_einstein_limit/quantum_scalar_radar/volume.json)
  - **Positive written-field ring Hamiltonian extension** · An addressed interferometer reads net face energy influx −0.0129025 ± 0.0000893 per exposure. Two phases have identical histories in the ring-energy and current algebra and change the vacancy and excess transport curvatures by 0.092388 and 0.011183. Their proper spacetime curvature and directional pressures require separate measurements. [Comparison study](../experiments/interval_echo_einstein_station/field_stress_curvature/README.md)
  - **Finite quantized Wilson–Maxwell field in a prescribed periodic box** · A one-quantum preparation has resolvable and anisotropic Maxwell pressure. Across 24 weak optical channels on each of three boxes its maximum change of propagation is 2.78 × 10⁻¹⁶. The same readers detect a supplied clock change. The finite optical commutator is state-independent on the enrolled states. [Comparison study](../../code/matched_einstein_limit/quantum_stress_control/README.md)
  - **Homogeneous semiclassical Einstein geometry coupled to six quantized real scalar oscillators** · In a semiclassical control with one scalar axis withheld from the energy reader, fixed-volume area infers a source-density change of 0.00674881 at radius 0.1. A separate meter measures 0.00674591 for that axis. This is a prepared bulk scalar mode. A non-luminous anomalous collar charge, its physical radius and universal coupling require independent measurements. [Comparison study](../../code/matched_einstein_limit/quantum_scalar_radar/partial_meter.json)
  - **Homogeneous semiclassical Einstein geometry with a bilinear quantum-field interaction** · At equal single-field density matrices, the measured cross covariance forecasts a timelike Einstein contrast of 0.00296540. Radar gives 0.00296837 and equal-volume area gives 0.00296947. With the bilinear interaction removed both geometry records coincide. The supplied classical metric dynamics couple to expected stress. [Comparison study](../../code/matched_einstein_limit/quantum_scalar_radar/species_transfer/correlation.json)
  - **Homogeneous finite-mode semiclassical Einstein–scalar Hamiltonian** · The Hamiltonian gives the three spatial equations and conserves volume times the time-constraint defect. The initialized regular solution obeys null balance in every direction. Diagonal radar and matter records bound the held all-sky error by 0.00000710 at the finest spacing, rising to 0.04111 without backreaction. This comparison supplies the ADM Hamiltonian and its matter coupling. [Comparison study](../../code/matched_einstein_limit/quantum_scalar_radar/null_sky.json)
  - **Interval Echo on the prepared two-head equilibrium** · A signed sum of twelve readings cancels all endpoint delays. Its four sender contributions have opposite signs for excess and vacancy probes under every positive set of sender gains. The same source permits reconstruction of the parked energy and modular response from independent material clicks. [Comparison study](../../code/matched_einstein_limit/source_implication/null_optics/README.md)
  - **Interval Echo on the prepared two-head equilibrium** · Three timeouts allow either charge to recover the same survey area. Full-rank native polynomial moments allow opposite intercell current responses with the same conserved launch charge and the same ordinary current calibration. An independently calibrated four-hop tetrahedron gives a pairing entropy response ratio between −0.005871 and −0.005732 where +1 is required. [Comparison study](../../code/matched_einstein_limit/PROOF.md)

## Clock dilation, three-dimensional fields, signal timing and bound-pair dynamics

Observers measure how a moving clock runs, whether a field varies along three independent spatial directions, and when a message can first be decoded. They also test whether separated loads form a lasting pair, where it travels and whether it returns, how its clock phase advances, and how its wave frequency depends on momentum.

### OBS-01

**Time dilation of a moving clock.** Measure speed independently. Read a returning finite patch’s evolving hand and its rest companion at home. Reproduce the clock forecast and its tolerance on held journeys, clock constructions and speeds.

On the torus five long-wave moving/rest residuals were at most 0.00371 against a 0.035 limit. A broad packet missed by 0.06546. Two clocks agreed on five journeys. Six bound-clock flights passed the finite-packet correction.

Criterion · [README.md](https://github.com/FloatingPragma/observer-patch-holography/blob/cea95a7e62df1059bc55b299339ea45c09159977/model/README.md#measured-on-simulated-observers)

- **Interval Echo · Partial.** Prepared moving and home hands agree with independent reciprocal optical timing on held 3/5, 5/3 and 7/7 schedules across three physical home links on each of two screens. At 8/8 the original three-bit hand fails the frozen band; the five-bit hand passes on the same itinerary. The coherent hand passes separate accepted-event comparisons. Timing-equivalent embeddings share one cohort. An independently generated light cone and a common rate across other physical clock mechanisms are open. ### OBS-02

**Three spatial directions in a field measured by observers.** Infer three independent directions and scales from timed patch records. Predict withheld field measurements across stations and refinements and test whether dropping any direction spoils them.

The torus supplied its three spatial routing axes. Nine stations predicted 120 held field values with RMS 0.001103. Omitting successive spatial derivatives raised RMS to 0.3123, 0.3246 and 0.3246. RER also recovered a three-dimensional Gram matrix from native cell records and, in a separate free-field inverse experiment, a supplied cube from eight relabelled channels.

Criterion · [README.md](https://github.com/FloatingPragma/observer-patch-holography/blob/cea95a7e62df1059bc55b299339ea45c09159977/model/README.md#measured-on-simulated-observers) · [README.md](https://github.com/FloatingPragma/observer-patch-holography/blob/374b2390801842d38de9b9c326e79a8ab29a6be5/model/observer_reconstruction/README.md)

- **Interval Echo** · **Demonstrated** · [Blind recovery of three programmed station directions](../experiments/seam_program/CLOCKED_FIELD.md). Full criterion · Partial. Relabelled local ports recover a rank-three Gram. On complete 20-, 80- and 320-cell screens, neighbouring patches decode all 144 held three-sign preparations. A short-range incidence survey plus the frozen hopping scale predicts 72 further words at held senders and previously untested dwell times. The signal is an occupation-probability contrast. Timing and addresses are supplied; a three-dimensional field with independently ranged physical scales and corresponding refined stations remains open. On a seven-station menu, every board has a five-site subset whose measured ranges exclude a three-dimensional Euclidean placement within 5%. On the programmed native scalar instrument anonymous one-period counts recover three binary station directions and predict 24 held clock-stopped distributions. The largest absolute discrepancy is 0.001022. Dropping any recovered binary direction forces an error of at least 0.11377. The controller supplies the spatial routing. ### OBS-03

**Earliest decodable message in observer time.** Choose a bit after setup. The receiver commits its answer by independently ranged early and late deadlines on its own patch clock. Test passive signals, every allowed relay and quiet controls. Record both accuracy and rare early clicks.

The torus passive receiver had early probability at most 5.878 × 10⁻³⁵, early balanced decoding at 50% and late decoding at 100%. An addressed relay decoded all 512 bits early. A pulse peak measures a different event from the first usable message.

Criterion · [README.md](https://github.com/FloatingPragma/observer-patch-holography/blob/cea95a7e62df1059bc55b299339ea45c09159977/model/README.md#measured-on-simulated-observers)

- **Interval Echo** · **Demonstrated** · [Passive echoes predict a one-way bit read on the same material clock](../experiments/interval_echo_message/passive_echo/README.md). Full criterion · Partial. On a 20-cell board the sender chooses its bit after setup. One double and the message single share the same native registers. With the hand three links from the receiving gateway, the local terminal address alone decodes 138,044 of 262,144 bits by the first recorded tick and 144,928 by the third, counting every timeout. The hand word alone gives accuracy consistent with chance in the passive arm. The single addressed relay scores 233,100 and 182,641 at these deadlines. A separate range bank records 50 and 588 passive marked round trips per 131,072 launches, or 612 and 3,274 with an addressed turnaround. The no-encoding arm scores near chance. Placing the hand on the gateway instead makes its tick record carry nearly all the bit discrimination. The experiment establishes local commitments and return distributions for two prechosen deadlines in a bounded intervention menu. It leaves the earliest usable signal across the full relay menu and a common physical speed unresolved. On the original passive stations an arbitrarily small positive advantage exists immediately in native exposure. On one 20-cell screen the addressed out-and-back service ranged three ports at two, four and six receiver ticks. A 52% decoding threshold chosen using the near pilot was fixed before testing the two farther ports. The near passive receiver decoded 137,471 of 262,144 bits by tick one. The middle decoded 132,275 at its forecast tick two and the held-out far port decoded 131,072 at tick three. Neither farther port qualified at any tested deadline through its forecast. Quiet outcomes and timeouts count. The addressed range specifies a service-dependent distance; passive ranging had zero marked returns at both farther ports. With one passive load and a terminal receiver, a separate exact bound quantifies the early tail. At exposure 0.01 on the 20-cell screen, at least 1,601 independent repetitions of the same bit are needed for 52% accuracy one port link away and 256,017,068 two links away, even for a joint quantum read. These exposure bounds do not supply observer-clock durations. A stricter stationary hand requires the double at port 2 on every successive tick. The message and both echo legs use the same passive two-load flight. On the held-out sender, 57 and zero marked returns per 2³⁰ starts at six ticks forecast 50.00371–50.01474% one-way bit accuracy at three ticks. The independent test gave 50.01152%. The simultaneous 99% upper bound on the best classical reading of the full single-launch records is below 51.856% across the twelve native settings. The exposed practical threshold was 52%. The result identifies a reciprocal probability law under this clock read and supplied interrogation cadence. On the separate one-load stations, port-resolved echoes predict all 520 one-way probability bins within the joint sampling limits. At native exposure four on 20 cells, direct local arrival on bit zero was 42.52% and labelled echoes predicted 42.54%. A coherent whole-cell echo gave a square-root estimate of 13.66%. The midpoint detector is part of the calibration. ### OBS-06

**Phase rate of a clock on the formed pair.** Read the same internal phase at several declared elapsed intervals using local reference records. Include visibility, rate uncertainty, source width, receiver aperture and quiet outcomes.

Halving the amplitude on each write quarters the predicted arrival contrast. The mean of any fixed complete two-word readout misses the positive RMS amplitude by at least one eighth of its full value at one of three strengths. The connected modular-generator contrast is positive, zero or negative under three different faithful references. A physical reference, cut count, conversion to mass and a geometry calibrated on ordinary stress are open. The scoped galaxy tests distinguish these gaps. The western Bullet lensing peak lies 162 kpc outside the region for two gas-centred monotone radial contributions. Outer shear in 22 galaxies exceeds central predictions from the two tested mass calibrations, with large cross shear. Matched-radius orbital data, lens-source weights and a predictive transport law are needed. A normalization from 35 inner lenses worsens the held median angle error on 18 other inner lenses from 11.6% to 15.8%. It also worsens the flat-speed prediction for the two bins in a 22-lens outer stack. The two-word source needs a signed fourth-power coefficient on measured local balls to enter Einstein stress. Its protected flight cannot select joint word weights through equilibration inside each word sector. With independent preparation tags on each of 20 ancestors, a native connected modular charge divided by the fourth power of routed radius gives 0.001713 and 0.001761 nats in home-range units on the two finest boards, and 0.001765 on doubling the finest radius. Far disjoint annuli have zero mixed charge. Near disjoint annuli have a mixed contrast of (0.9703 ± 0.0883) × 10⁻⁹ nats. Ordered pair admissions account for the scaling. The routed lengths bypass the sources and the tag and modular reference are supplied. A deep scale calibrated on 74 SPARC galaxies gives limiting speeds of 58.0–69.3 km/s for six external gas-rich diffuse galaxies with measured speeds of 19–37 km/s. Under the finite-radius additive acceleration law, the AGC 114905 outer ring gives 62.91 km/s against 26.94 ± 1.83 at 9.95 kpc. The comparison uses the published disk reductions and no dwarf refit. For the spherical positive-density finite-edge halo with universal mass cap from AGC 114905, even the generous inclination, dwarf-speed and lens-mass settings permit weighted excess surface densities up to 9.94 and 9.74 solar masses per square parsec in KiDS-bright and GAMA. The measured densities are 17.30 ± 0.69 and 22.73 ± 2.99 in the same units (reported statistical errors). A broader shared sublinear spherical profile gives upper bounds of 11.36 and 11.18. This transfer rejects that common cap on the tested weak-field lensing prescription. The relativistic annular stress closure requires a pressure prescription, conserved stress and boundary data. Reversing the calibration on the untrimmed spherical branch gives shear scales of 1.530 and 2.813 × 10⁻¹⁰ m/s² at nominal inputs, while the AGC 114905 outer ring gives an upper scale of 2.699 × 10⁻¹² m/s² if this branch applies there. Conditional native modular variances change with the source history at fixed stored energy and coincide across the tested 64- and 130-unit energy bins. These records supply no per-baryonic-mass variance or proper cut density. A free mobile load generates cross-seam correlations from two independently armed heads. A fresh probe detects them with both local marginals held fixed. The correlation and the probe response reverse sign between the two boards. Nine separate hydrogen orbits outside five effective radii enclose at least 90% of modelled baryons. At catalogue masses and distances, NGC 4262 and NGC 3626 require 4.443 quoted speed errors to admit a common increasing sublinear excess-mass profile. At an 80% enclosure and low-acceleration cut, one lens-admitted linear profile accommodates three orbits within 0.575 quoted distance and speed errors. The same linear comparison needs 3.015 errors on the nine objects. These survey results do not identify an endogenous stress scale. Across six individual bins the linear-profile witness gives covariance distances squared of 24.38 in KiDS-bright and 12.89 in GAMA at bin centres. Unknown positive weights inside the bins lower these distances to 4.39 and 8.05. Free-flight endpoint counts distinguish genuine collar correlations from independently prepared equal marginals. An equal-energy stationary seam source fails the ordinary-source probe calibration. The geometry and full null stress of that source remain unmeasured. ## OpenWave empirical criteria

Assess the 31 OpenWave physical targets locally on every registered RER law. The public M10 column at the cited revision pins Interval Echo. OpenWave partially validated Row 4 and marked the other 30 rows as not yet tested. Each RER grade describes the measurement and scope named in its entry.

### OW-01

**Charge quantization.** Exhibit an integer topological electric charge and exclude division of one unit between two half-charged excitations.

Criterion · [MODELS.md L101](https://github.com/openwave-labs/openwave/blob/d4fc3e4046c472f4ad38468df8dddfa1f97bac97/MODELS.md#L101)

- **Interval Echo · Open.** Two compact vacancy modes share a single missing load over cells 5, 11 and 23 owner steps apart on three boards. Paired contact reads distinguish that state from independent preparations with the same local means on each board in a fresh precision replicate. At every free appointment the reduced state of the gateways and blank collars on either tested enclosing cut equals the filled host, even on a branch with the vacancy wholly inside. A single load and a doubled load also persist on empty hosts. The measurements select neither a topological electric unit nor an electric charge law excluding two half-charge daughters. [SPEC.md](../candidate_laws/interval_echo/SPEC.md) · [README.md](../candidate_laws/interval_echo/openwave/row_01_charge/README.md) · [results.json](../candidate_laws/interval_echo/openwave/row_01_charge/results.json)
  - **Written-field Laplacian extension** · In 18 half-transfer fixtures the sharp regional head flux follows the whole signed load. A head-independent control has 50% mismatch. The integer step is supplied by the alphabets. [Comparison study](../candidate_laws/interval_echo/openwave/electron_field/README.md#which-preparation-carries-the-charge)
- **Contrast memory · Open.** Integer port loads and their conserved populations are measured in the coherent blank sector. Establishing an electric topological charge and excluding physical half-charge daughters requires a charge readout.
- **Private record walk · Open.** Awaiting candidate-specific assessment.
- **Scalar channel · Open.** Electric topological charge and a forbidden splitting channel have yet to be identified.

### OW-02

**Electron rest energy (mass).** Select a localized state dynamically. With its energy anchored at 511 keV, predict and independently measure another physical quantity fixed by that anchor.

Criterion · [MODELS.md L102](https://github.com/openwave-labs/openwave/blob/d4fc3e4046c472f4ad38468df8dddfa1f97bac97/MODELS.md#L102)

- **Interval Echo** · **Demonstrated** · [Boundary recoil and binding measured on the same two-load preparation](../candidate_laws/interval_echo/openwave/row_03_clock/moving_pair/recoil/README.md). Full criterion · Partial. Gateway monitoring holds a compact vacancy. With a material clock and six-seam storage, separate joint reads give 35 for one vacancy and 70 for two separated copies. A changed pair interaction shifts the two-copy reading. A vacancy and excess give 35 and 67 with this reference, or 43 each with five-seam storage. Independent storage readings account for the difference. A local phase flip lowers the mean vacancy energy. A local pulse releases charge from that same held vacancy. On 20, 80 and 320 cells the native one-vacancy spectrum excludes the relativistic recoil energy at pc = 511 keV with the six-seam anchor. Its identification with an electron rest threshold remains open. Preparing two vacancies and an excess gives a repulsively bound charged trimer. The upper-band configuration stays intact with probability at least 0.5745 at all exposures on the 20-cell board and 0.7298 on both refinements. Its host-referenced recoil target exceeds the whole sector ceiling on every board. Internal rearrangement is observed and translational inertia remains unresolved. A separate energy read on these ordered-pair launches gives 212.988–213.010 in empty-host exchange units. The full two-amount sector has spectral width at most 44, excluding an internal beat at that formation energy. Its inertial mass needs an independent motional measurement. On the same ordered pair that conveys a phase, a calibrated boundary impulse produces a current predicted by an independent swap measurement. At one clock unit the opposite-kick half-difference is 0.00828–0.00838 across three placements. Each freely chosen later adjacency reading succeeds with probability at least 0.84319. An exchange-only comparator calibrated to the same 213 formation energy and clock gives a different recoil. The current is measured per exchange time; a physical inertial mass requires its own ruler and momentum calibration. ### OW-03

**de Broglie clock (Zitterbewegung).** Measure an internally initiated, frequency-rigid oscillation whose angular frequency equals the measured rest energy divided by ℏ.

Criterion · [MODELS.md L103](https://github.com/openwave-labs/openwave/blob/d4fc3e4046c472f4ad38468df8dddfa1f97bac97/MODELS.md#L103)

- **Interval Echo** · **Demonstrated** · [The relative phase of two bound loads reaches three remote seam detectors](../candidate_laws/interval_echo/openwave/row_03_clock/moving_pair/README.md). Full criterion · Partial. A sharp vacancy with monitored gateways gives a free internal beat at 2.237 and 2.232 per independently timed exchange unit on 80 and 320 cells. Halving the contrast shifts its mean energy relative to the uniform host from 34.62 to 18.62 while leaving the beat at 2.234. The full blank one-vacancy spectrum is contained in [32,54] on all three boards, so an internal frequency equal to the separately measured 35-unit vacancy transfer is excluded in this sector. Even without monitoring, a sharp vacancy on each larger board contributes a cosine at √5 with coefficient at least 1/40 in its free return probability. The material timer and terminal reads are supplied apparatus. On the exposed 17- and 20-dimer closed strands, pair-transition spectroscopy gives a rest frequency near 15. On those free rings every energy difference, including differences between flip sectors, lies within 12.421 or 14.613 units respectively of a multiple of 32. A single-copy internal reading with a fixed detector therefore cannot oscillate at any frequency in the measured rest interval [14.99, 15.01]. The one-dimer record resolves a pair beat near 32 and a planted line at 15 on a changed law. On uniformly filled material with blank or zero-contrast memories, the primitive seam operations and sharp reads preserve the filled subspace under classical feedback. A prepared neutral pair can carry a 32-unit relative phase that those reads cannot detect. An external coherent analyzer resolves it. For an entire apparatus at fixed total excess load Q, a uniform energy shift λQ leaves every charge-preserving transcript invariant. The vacancy and excess energy assignments (35, 67) and (51, 51) both give neutral energy 102. These readings leave the individual rest energy undetermined. On a unit-filled host, one coin reading starts a free local beat on a written neutral seam. At native contrast eight, the even and odd preparations give angular frequencies 31.995 and 33.001 in independent dimer-clock units. A separate instrument resolves the excited formation lines at 32.007 and 32.990. The same preparations have mean energies 15.982 and 16.467 because half the weight is on the zero-energy branch. The mark has no free transfer between seams. This measures a stationary excitation gap; assigning a particle rest energy requires independent inertia. On the all-open 20-cell screen a bound pair of unequal loads carries its prepared ordering phase to detectors across three owner boundaries. The peak intact arrival probabilities are 0.48–1.25 per million launches. Fresh phase comparisons at each bridge exclude the isolated clock rate even with varying visibility and an arbitrary fixed phase origin. A separate energy read on these ordered-pair launches gives 212.988–213.010 in empty-host exchange units. The full two-amount sector has spectral width at most 44, excluding an internal beat at that formation energy. Its inertial mass needs an independent motional measurement. ### OW-04

**Particle stability (Derrick escape).** Release the prepared particle state and measure its persistence under free evolution. Identify the excitation and the domain of the measurement. The separate SM-e entry assesses its identification as an electron.

Criterion · [MODELS.md L104](https://github.com/openwave-labs/openwave/blob/753232cac4976ab2ca208eb99d73fe7ac88ef333/MODELS.md#L104) · [MODELS.md L438](https://github.com/openwave-labs/openwave/blob/753232cac4976ab2ca208eb99d73fe7ac88ef333/MODELS.md#L438) · [745](https://github.com/openwave-labs/openwave/pull/745#pullrequestreview-6005365373) · [746](https://github.com/openwave-labs/openwave/pull/746)

- **Interval Echo** · **Demonstrated** · [Free persistence of a prepared pair](../candidate_laws/interval_echo/openwave/row_04/README.md). OpenWave · [⚠️ Partial](https://github.com/openwave-labs/openwave/pull/745#pullrequestreview-6005365373). Resubmission · [HOLD](../../openwave/interval_echo/row_04/CHECKLIST.md). Free persistence is demonstrated locally for a prepared pair of unassigned species. At held exposure 37 the launch patch has adjacent-pair probability 0.98027 at κ = 8 versus 0.20291 at κ = 0. Seeded records gave 4,028 versus 824 detections in 4,096 draws per arm. For that cell-13 launch the home probability stays at least 0.94 through exposure 37. OpenWave reproduced the probabilities and partially validated the historical pin, whose design search compared strengths using persistence. A later law uses the same signed-record comparison for exchange parity and eight incident witnesses on a shielded standard cell. Its magnitude-one energy is exactly 8, reproducing the binary-pair generator. With a one-port preparation calibrated by stationarity, its magnitude-two energy is exactly 32 and the complete seam generator agrees. A direct simulation recorded 104 transitions from lower modes into magnitude two. The 17-offer digital realization also passes the phase-gate tolerance and all 24 material reunion comparisons. The target was known when the comparator and uniform deployment were chosen. Using fair parity instead approaches 4. The portable contact instrument distinguishes κ = 8 from all eight other published strengths and a pair-only rival before release. For adjacent launches on the stated federation class the probability of adjacency anywhere is at least 5/6 at every exposure. The cell-13 home-pair probability is at least 0.96325 through exposure 37. On the 20-cell board, all 600 internal launch seams clear both sampling forecasts. At exposure 37, 24 have home probability below 0.94. At κ = 8 the 630 distinct-load band levels lie below the two-free-load rest reference. At κ = −8 they lie above the free exchange band. A separate source equates an empty primitive collar bank’s differential frequency to the material coefficient and obtains κ = 8. The zero-strength donor gives the same calibration fringe. This equality was chosen with the persistence results known. The historical pair's coupling has no independent selection in the published sweep. The archived clock and vacuum fringes agree at κ = ½ and 8 while the original home-pair probabilities at exposure 37 differ by more than 0.56. A distinct single-load source passes the free home-cell test for every real coupling on all 420 cells, including preparation exhaustion. Its all-time joint home probability is at least 0.999940596800. OpenWave awarded Partial to the historical pair. The single-load registration is prepared for owner review. Its changed state and detector require adjudication and its task-selected confirmation is pending. The prepared-pair proposal in OpenWave PR #749 uses eleven written memories. The connected-pair certificate applies to 1,992 eligible layouts across all 420 cells. During free flight each neutral mark stays on its seam. Erasing one mark with a native echo before acquisition gives a different terminal record from a permanent cut under the same intervention. At exposure 37 the total variation across the 31 outcomes is 0.56510 at zero coupling and 0.06569 at the registered coupling 8. At 8 the probabilities of a home reading are 0.99571 and 0.99611, respectively. These calculated probabilities distinguish a writable contact from a permanent cut. A stationary programmable trap remains consistent with them. The single-load full-credit filing is on HOLD. ### OW-05

**Magnetic moment μ (g ≈ 2, magnetostatics).** Measure a static magnetic dipole sourced by internal circulation in the stationary electron, its gyromagnetic factor near two and its Larmor response.

Criterion · [MODELS.md L105](https://github.com/openwave-labs/openwave/blob/d4fc3e4046c472f4ad38468df8dddfa1f97bac97/MODELS.md#L105)

- **Interval Echo** · **Demonstrated** · [Stationary vacancy current and its precession in an applied flux](../candidate_laws/interval_echo/openwave/row_05_dipole/README.md). Full criterion · Partial. Each owner on the 20-, 80- and 320-cell federations supports a compact stationary vacancy with signed material current. The measured precession frequency of a tilted current vector is 0.011053 per exchange unit, with 99% interval [0.010940,0.011161], against 0.011049 from the zero-field moments and applied flux. A probe constrained outside the source cell has exactly the same channel for either current and for no source. This strictly compact degenerate band has zero recoil under the owner momentum kick. An orthogonal, dispersive orbital carries the vacancy and its direction to another owner, with one-hop signed moment +0.001409 versus −0.000485 per launch. Its broad energy distribution separates this mobile preparation from the precisely stationary compact band. A static exterior dipole field and independent finite inertial mass and spin standards for an electron gyromagnetic factor are unresolved. On a ten-port orbital belt, flux splitting and independent small-momentum recoil gave g_orb = 0.63327 [0.61951, 0.64710] at simultaneous 99% coverage. Using the first finite recoil gap instead gave 0.70804 and missed the held second gap. These ratios exclude g near two for that orbital. In a two-owner aperture a reflection makes the complete exterior measurement record independent of the circulation direction even when an excess probe enters the source. Opening a second gateway gives a sign-sensitive scattering signal. At exposure 32 the probability of finding the excess at exterior port 48 is 0.40612 for one circulation and 0.42873 for its reverse. The corresponding joint probabilities of recovering the original circulating mode and finding the probe outside are 0.22211 and 0.23577. A static dipole field and a physical electron gyromagnetic factor remain unresolved. ### OW-07

**Spin-½ statistics (720° double cover).** Interfere a full rotation, or a two-core exchange, with a reference and recover a minus sign. A dynamically selected half-turn discriminated from a polar control provides intermediate evidence.

Criterion · [MODELS.md L107](https://github.com/openwave-labs/openwave/blob/753232cac4976ab2ca208eb99d73fe7ac88ef333/MODELS.md#L107)

- **Interval Echo** · **Demonstrated** · [Measured phase from filtering two commuting spatial half-turns](../experiments/interval_echo_angular/charged_orientation/projected_turns/PROOF.md). Full criterion · Open. On the freely arriving signed orbital, a controlled full template turn and opposite orders of perpendicular half-turns give positive relative phase. Two addressed orbital packets exchange with +SWAP. The same apparatus resolves the imposed spinorial and odd-permutation signs. This orbital construction fails the requested double-cover test. Two commuting native spatial half-turns on the twelve-port template give a negative relative order phase on the quarter of launches that pass both code filters. Repeating one half-turn twice gives positive phase, including on the accepted branch. For any coherent code with input-independent success and commuting full half-turns, anticommuting accepted unitaries require the two success probabilities to sum to at most one. The measured code attains the bound using a supplied filter and coherent reference. Two frame-preserving guided exchanges of energy-filtered trimers give phase +1. A further improper reflection gives the negative port. In transit the probability of two connected triples falls from 99.867% to 7.636%. All 15 proper template half-turns measurably change the selected trimer’s address distribution relative to idle, including its three closest axes.
